 6633:     def _validate_replan_evidence(
 6634:         self,
 6635:         run_dir: Path,
 6636:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6637:         state: dict[str, Any],
 6638:         *,
 6639:         historical: bool = False,
 6640:     ) -> str:
 6641:         evidence = reason["evidence"]
 6642:         artifact_ref = evidence.get("artifact_ref")
 6643:         if not isinstance(artifact_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 6644:             r"(?:request_change_[0-9a-f]{32}|sol_review_[0-9]+|replan_evidence_[0-9]+)\.json",
 6645:             artifact_ref,
 6646:         ):
 6647:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not a bounded host artifact", "blocked")
 6648:         artifact = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6649:         try:
 6650:             artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6651:         except ValueError as exc:
 6652:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6653:         if not artifact.is_file():
 6654:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6655:         value = read_json(artifact)
 6656:         code = reason["code"]
 6657:         kind = REPLAN_REASON_KINDS[code]
 6658: 
 6659:         if code == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 6660:             config = state.get("config")
 6661:             request_ref = evidence.get("request_ref")
 6662:             request_path = (run_dir / request_ref).resolve() if isinstance(request_ref, str) else None
 6663:             request_digest_ok = (
 6664:                 request_path is not None
 6665:                 and request_path.is_file()
 6666:                 and isinstance(evidence.get("request_sha256"), str)
 6667:                 and hashlib.sha256(request_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == evidence["request_sha256"]
 6668:             )
 6669:             valid = (
 6670:                 artifact_ref == request_ref
 6671:                 and (historical or request_ref == state.get("request_ref"))
 6672:                 and request_digest_ok
 6673:                 and isinstance(config, dict)
 6674:                 and isinstance(value, dict)
 6675:                 and set(value) == {"artifact_type", "run_id", "request_ref", "request", "requested_files", "reason"}
 6676:                 and value.get("artifact_type") == "user_request_change_v1"
 6677:                 and value.get("run_id") == state.get("run_id")
 6678:                 and value.get("request_ref") == request_ref
 6679:                 and (historical or value.get("request") == config.get("request"))
 6680:                 and (historical or value.get("requested_files") == config.get("requested_files"))
 6681:                 and isinstance(value.get("requested_files"), list)
 6682:                 and isinstance(value.get("reason"), dict)
 6683:                 and value["reason"].get("code") == code
 6684:                 and value["reason"].get("evidence", {}).get("artifact_ref") == artifact_ref
 6685:                 and value["reason"].get("evidence", {}).get("kind") == kind
 6686:                 and value["reason"].get("evidence", {}).get("verified") is True
 6687:             )
 6688:             if not valid:
 6689:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not the current immutable user request artifact", "blocked")
 6690:         elif code in {
 6691:             "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED",
 6692:             "REQUIRED_GAP_UNCOVERED",
 6693:             "ARCHITECTURE_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT",
 6694:         } and re.fullmatch(r"sol_review_[0-9]+\.json", artifact_ref):
 6695:             identity_fields = (
 6696:                 "run_id",
 6697:                 "master_plan_ref",
 6698:                 "request_ref",
 6699:                 "request_sha256",
 6700:                 "task_id",
 6701:                 "base_commit",
 6702:                 "candidate_id",
 6703:                 "candidate_ref",
 6704:                 "review_ref",
 6705:                 "verification_ref",
 6706:                 "verification",
 6707:                 "verification_attestation_sha256",
 6708:                 "sequence",
 6709:             )
 6710:             if any(field not in evidence for field in identity_fields):
 6711:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence Sol reason lacks complete identity", "blocked")
 6712:             request_ref = evidence["request_ref"]
 6713:             request_path = (run_dir / request_ref).resolve() if isinstance(request_ref, str) else None
 6714:             if (
 6715:                 request_path is None
 6716:                 or not request_path.is_file()
 6717:                 or not isinstance(evidence["request_sha256"], str)
 6718:                 or hashlib.sha256(request_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != evidence["request_sha256"]
 6719:             ):
 6720:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence request identity is stale", "blocked")
 6721:             candidate_ref = evidence["candidate_ref"]
 6722:             if not re.fullmatch(r"candidate_[0-9]+\.json", candidate_ref):
 6723:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no bounded candidate lineage", "blocked")
 6724:             candidate_path = (run_dir / candidate_ref).resolve()
 6725:             if not candidate_path.is_file():
 6726:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence candidate artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6727:             candidate = read_json(candidate_path)
 6728:             plan_ref = evidence["master_plan_ref"]
 6729:             plan_path = (run_dir / plan_ref).resolve() if isinstance(plan_ref, str) else None
 6730:             config = state.get("config")
 6731:             if plan_path is None or not re.fullmatch(r"plans/[0-9a-f]{64}\.json", plan_ref.replace("\\", "/")):
 6732:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no bounded master plan lineage", "blocked")
 6733:             if not plan_path.is_file():
 6734:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence master plan artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6735:             plan = read_json(plan_path)
 6736:             canonical_plan = json.dumps(plan, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
 6737:             if hashlib.sha256(canonical_plan.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != plan_ref.split("/")[-1][:-5]:
 6738:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence master plan lineage is stale", "blocked")
 6739:             if not isinstance(config, dict) or not isinstance(config.get("requested_files"), list):

 6744:             if task is None:
 6745:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence task identity is stale", "blocked")
 6746:             if (

 6755:             review = value

 7037:     def _block_replan_exhaustion(
 7038:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], *, attempts: int, reason: dict[str, Any] | None = None
 7039:     ) -> None:
 7040:         block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"

 7059:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 7060:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 7061:     ) -> str:
 7062:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 7063:         artifact_ref = reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
 7064:         artifact_path = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 7065:         try:
 7066:             artifact_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7067:         except ValueError as exc:
 7068:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 7069:         if not artifact_path.is_file():
 7070:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 7071:         if reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 7072:             reason["evidence"]["request_ref"] = artifact_ref
 7073:             reason["evidence"]["request_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(artifact_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
 7074:         evidence_hash = self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state)
 7075:         reason["evidence"]["artifact_sha256"] = evidence_hash
 7076:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 7077:         if self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir) >= 2:
 7078:             self._block_replan_exhaustion(
 7079:                 run_dir,
 7080:                 state,
 7081:                 attempts=self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir),
 7082:                 reason=reason,
 7083:             )
 7084:         record = {
 7085:             "schema_version": 1,
 7086:             "sequence": attempt,
 7087:             "code": reason["code"],
 7088:             "evidence": reason["evidence"],
 7089:         }
 7090:         serialized = (
 7091:             json.dumps(record, indent=2, sort_keys=True).replace("\n", os.linesep) + os.linesep
 7092:         ).encode("utf-8")
 7093:         record_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(serialized).hexdigest()
 7094:         reason_ref = f"replan_history/{record_sha256}.json"
 7095:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 7096:         if reason_path.is_file() and reason_path.read_bytes() != serialized:
 7097:             raise FactoryError("replan history content collision", "blocked")
 7098:         if not reason_path.is_file():
 7099:             atomic_json(reason_path, record)
 7100:         history = state.get("replan_history")
 7101:         if not isinstance(history, list):
 7102:             history = []
 7103:         history = list(history)
 7104:         history.append(
 7105:             {
 7106:                 "sequence": attempt,
 7107:                 "record_ref": reason_ref,
 7108:                 "record_sha256": record_sha256,
 7109:                 "code": reason["code"],
 7110:             }
 7111:         )
 7112:         self._save(
 7113:             run_dir,
 7114:             state,
 7115:             "planning",
 7116:             replan_reason_ref=reason_ref,
 7117:             replan_history=history,
 7118:         )
 7119:         return reason_ref

 7121:     def request_replan(
 7122:         self,
 7123:         identifier: str,
 7124:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 7125:         *,
 7126:         objective: str | None = None,
 7127:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 7128:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7129:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 7130:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 7131:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 7132:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 7133:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 7134:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 7135:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 7136:             if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 7137:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
 7138:             self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 7139:             if normalized_reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 7140:                 if not isinstance(objective, str) or not objective.strip() or requested_files is None:
 7141:                     raise FactoryError(
 7142:                         "user objective/scope replan requires replacement objective and authorized files",
 7143:                         "input",
 7144:                     )

 7147:                 ensure_paths_confined(repo, replacement_files)
 7148:                 ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo, replacement_files)

 7150:                 state["config"] = asdict(config)
 7151:                 change_ref = f"request_change_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.json"

 7164:                 )
 7165:                 state["request_ref"] = change_ref
 7166:                 state["request_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256((run_dir / change_ref).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

  365:             self.model_calls.append(model)
  366:             self.reviewer_prompts.append(prompt)
  367:             verification = json.loads(prompt.split("Input:\n", 1)[1])["verification"]
  368:             digest = re.search(r'"sha256":\s*"([0-9a-f]{64})"', prompt).group(1)
  369:             base_commit = re.search(r'"base_commit":\s*"([0-9a-f]{40})"', prompt).group(1)
  370:             candidate_id = re.search(r'"candidate_id":\s*"([0-9a-f]{40})"', prompt).group(1)
  371:             global_match = re.search(r'"global_criteria_ids":\s*\[(.*?)\]', prompt, re.S)
  372:             global_ids = re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"', global_match.group(1))
  373:             acceptance_id = re.search(r'"acceptance_criteria_ids":\s*\[\s*"([^"]+)"', prompt).group(1)
  374:             value = {
  375:                 "decision": "APPROVE", "reviewed_diff_sha256": digest,
  376:                 "base_commit": base_commit, "candidate_id": candidate_id,
  377:                 "verification": verification,
  378:                 "criteria": [
  379:                     *({"criterion": item, "passed": True} for item in global_ids),
  380:                     {"criterion": acceptance_id, "passed": True},
  381:                 ],
  382:                 "findings": [], "required_changes": [], "route": "none",
  383:             }
  384:             atomic_json(usage_path, {"model": model, "completed": True, "failed": False})
  385:             write_text(output_path, json.dumps(value))
  386:             return value
  387:         return super().call(role=role, provider=provider, model=model, prompt=prompt,
  388:                             usage_path=usage_path, output_path=output_path, timeout=timeout)
  389:

  954:         config = self.config()

 1133:     def test_multi_replan_history_survives_user_replacement_and_blocks_earlier_tamper(self):
 1134:         roles = MultiReplanRoles(replan_codes=["ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED"])
 1135:         builder = ScopeChangingBuilder()
 1136:         engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
 1137:         config = self.config()

 1150:                 interrupted["value"] = True
 1151:                 raise KeyboardInterrupt("pause before user replacement replan")

 1153:

 1183:     def test_multi_replan_history_revalidates_earlier_hermes_evidence(self):
 1184:         roles = HermesThenUserRoles()
 1185:         builder = ScopeChangingBuilder()
 1186:         engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
 1187:         config = self.config()
 1188:         config.requested_files = ["calc.py", "test_calc.py", "new.py"]
 1189:         config.test_command = f'"{sys.executable}" -c "pass"'
 1190:         (self.repo / "new.py").write_text("replacement = 0\n", encoding="utf-8")
 1191:         subprocess.run(["git", "add", "new.py"], cwd=self.repo, check=True)
 1192:         subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-qm", "add replacement fixture"], cwd=self.repo, check=True)
 1193:         identifier = engine.plan(config)
 1194:         original_execute_one = engine._execute_one_task
 1195:         pause_target = {"completed": 1}
 1196: 
 1197:         def pause_after_commit(*args, **kwargs):
 1198:             state = original_execute_one(*args, **kwargs)
 1199:             if len(state.get("completed_commits", [])) == pause_target["completed"]:
 1200:                 pause_target["completed"] = 2
 1201:                 raise KeyboardInterrupt("pause for Hermes replan evidence")
 1202:             return state
 1203: 
 1204:         with patch.object(engine, "_execute_one_task", side_effect=pause_after_commit):
 1205:             with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):

 5699:         # Keep the exact Runner result identity outside the mutable JSON fields.
 5700:         # The caller carries this returned snapshot through the review gate and
 5701:         # persists its digest in run state for safe resume.
 5702:         result["artifact_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(result_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    log = run_dir / \"test_1.log\"
    write_text(log, \"runner ok\\n\")
    result = {
        \"sequence\": 1,
        \"artifact_sequence\": 1,
        \"command\": \"python -m pytest -q\",
        \"returncode\": 0,
        \"passed\": True,
        \"log\": str(log.resolve()),
        \"log_sha256\": hashlib.sha256(log.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
    }
    result_path = run_dir / \"test_1.json\"
    atomic_json(result_path, result)
    original_sha = hashlib.sha256(result_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    result[\"artifact_sha256\"] = original_sha
    result_path.write_bytes(result_path.read_bytes() + b\" \\r\\n\")

    try:
        att = engine._create_verification_attestation(
            run_dir, result, task_id=\"T1\", base_commit=\"1\"*40, candidate_id=\"2\"*40
        )
        engine._validate_verification_attestation(
            run_dir, att[\"attestation_ref\"], attestation_sha256=att[\"attestation_sha256\"],
            expected_task_id=\"T1\", expected_base_commit=\"1\"*40,
            expected_candidate_id=\"2\"*40, expected_command=\"python -m pytest -q\"
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        print(json.dumps({\"accepted\": False, \"error\": f\"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\"}))
    else:
        print(json.dumps({
            \"accepted\": True,
            \"runner_snapshot_sha256\": original_sha,
            \"tampered_result_sha256\": tampered_sha,
            \"attested_result_sha256\": att[\"result_artifact_sha256\"],
            \"snapshot_digest_ignored\": att[\"result_artifact_sha256\"] "'!= result["artifact_sha256"]
        }))
